Current remote OMEMO/JET Jingle S5B file-transfer note: on 2026-05-27 `scripts/run-remote-omemo-jingle-s5b-smoke.sh` ran on the Linux workspace and then on the Windows MSYS2 VM against `dinox.im` with two redacted disposable DinoX accounts; both runs passed with `remote-omemo1-jingle-s5b-send-receive-ok`. Current Windows packaged GUI OMEMO2/JET receiver/open/export status: on 2026-06-01 the Windows VM rebuilt the clean gate checkout and refreshed `dist`, then Linux drove a stricter OMEMO2 sender-only harness plus a temporary external receiver v2-only PEP guard. The guard now requires disposable-account opt-in, a target receiver OMEMO2 device id, ready/done coordination files, pre-existing OMEMO1/OMEMO2 `current` PEP items, no PubSub node reconfiguration, restore readback, exact `current`-item reads instead of `request_all(max_items=1)`, and sender-visible empty OMEMO1 plus exact OMEMO2 bundle checks before sending. This did not close a normal DinoX GUI receiver proof: the packaged DinoX receiver republished its legacy OMEMO1 device list while online, so the sender timed out waiting for stable sender-visible v2-only PubSub state before any file send. That fail-closed result is the correct boundary for a dual OMEMO1/OMEMO2 DinoX receiver, and it is not a current DinoX-to-DinoX file-exchange blocker. A future OMEMO2/JET receiver/open/export proof requires a supported product DinoX-to-DinoX v2-only receiver mode. Kaidan 0.15.0 was inspected and remains out of the current pipeline: the installed Flatpak exposes no suitable CLI or D-Bus control surface, its local source registers QXmpp HTTP/encrypted HTTP file-sharing providers rather than a Jingle S5B file-transfer provider, its DOAP metadata lists XEP-0384 and XEP-0447 but not XEP-0234/XEP-0260/XEP-0261, and the 2026-06-01 XMPP software page lists XEP-0234 and XEP-0261 as missing. The PEP guard is only a disposable-account harness for controlled OMEMO2-only state and must not be used to claim normal DinoX GUI receiver behavior.

OpenPGP Gate 4 note: OpenPGP file transfer is not an encrypted Jingle/JET variant in the current product path. It must stay on HTTP/SFS, with the uploaded `.pgp` payload transferred through XEP-0363/HTTP and the HTTP body URL protected inside the selected OpenPGP-encrypted XEP-0374 or XEP-0027 message body. The `scripts/run-remote-openpgp-private-muc-http-smoke.sh --direct-sender-only` mode is the DinoX-to-DinoX one-to-one XEP-0374 sender for a separately controlled packaged Windows GUI receiver; `--direct-legacy-xep0027-sender-only` is the matching one-to-one legacy XEP-0027 sender. Their environment should name sender/receiver JIDs, the sender password, a sender secret-key file, the receiver public-key file and expected fingerprints, but never document or commit secret material. If a local GUI orchestration keeps the sender XEP-0373 key published for receiver fetch, that is a disposable-test-account PEP mutation requiring `DINOX_REMOTE_OPENPGP_HTTP_FILE_ALLOW_PEP_MUTATION=1` and is not a normal-account operation. XEP-0027 uses legacy email-UID key material plus account/contact legacy state and the direct sender waits for live verified signed-presence activity from the receiver before sending; XEP-0374 uses XMPP-UID key material plus XEP-0373/XEP-0374 state. The receiver conversation must be OpenPGP-selected with `encryption=PGP`; an unencrypted receiver chat does not count. On 2026-06-01 the packaged Windows GUI receiver independently completed receive-store, default-open and Save-as export with `provider=0`, `encryption=PGP`, protocol `openpgp:xep0374` and marker `remote-openpgp-packaged-windows-gui-receiver-open-export-ok`, so this gate is now closed for DinoX-to-DinoX OpenPGP HTTP/SFS receiver/open/export in the XEP-0374 path. OMEMO2/JET pipeline gates stay DinoX-to-DinoX; the normal DinoX-to-DinoX encrypted Jingle GUI receiver/open/export product path remains OMEMO1/JET.

Encrypted account-Tor HTTP/SFS note: on 2026-06-02 the env-backed OMEMO and OpenPGP private-MUC HTTP runners gained `*_MUC_ACCOUNT_PROXY_TYPE=none|socks5|tor` plus optional host/port validation, applied to every smoke account before login. With a real local Tor SOCKS listener at `127.0.0.1:9050`, Linux/headless DinoX-to-DinoX passed OMEMO1 seeded, OMEMO1 PubSub-discovered, OpenPGP XEP-0374 seeded, OpenPGP XEP-0374 PubSub-discovered and legacy OpenPGP XEP-0027 private-MUC HTTP/SFS send/receive via account-Tor. The freshly recloned Windows MSYS2 checkout at `b083c085` then rebuilt, refreshed `dist`, started a temporary local Tor SOCKS listener on `127.0.0.1:19050`, and passed the same five account-Tor smokes. Both platforms printed `remote-omemo1-private-muc-http-file-send-receive-ok`, `remote-omemo1-pubsub-private-muc-http-file-send-receive-ok`, `remote-openpgp-private-muc-http-file-send-receive-ok`, `remote-openpgp-pubsub-private-muc-http-file-send-receive-ok` and `remote-openpgp-xep0027-private-muc-http-file-send-receive-ok`. This closes the Linux/headless and Windows-MSYS2 encrypted account-Tor HTTP/SFS proof for OMEMO1 and OpenPGP; it deliberately does not claim OMEMO2, packaged GUI account-Tor HTTP/SFS or additional foreign-client behavior.

Durable/restart/MAM slice note: on 2026-06-02 the production-critical durable slice was narrowed to restart-safe terminalization plus retry, large-stream store coverage and server-persistence lookup. `FileTransferStorage` now converts legacy/stale persisted `IN_PROGRESS` rows to `FAILED` at startup; completed transfers remain available through their persisted local path, while interrupted Jingle live sessions are not falsely byte-resumed. Failed sent transfers with a persisted local at-rest copy can retry upload from that local copy without the original source file, and the default file UI exposes a sent-transfer "Retry upload" action. Local tests now cover successful retry from the encrypted local copy, cancelled retry keeping the copy failed, stale restart recovery, send-retry preconditions, >1 MiB outgoing/received stream storage with bounded 8192-byte read chunks, and 1:1 MAM/server-id lookup for messages stored with a sender resource while the conversation is bare. `Message.Marked.SENT` is still a local stream-send result; this slice does not add XEP-0198 ack waiting. Server persistence proof remains MAM/stanza-id or server-id replay into the stored message/source linkage. Encrypted Tor-onion variants are deliberately postponed because they build on this durable baseline; a product v2-only OMEMO2/JET receiver mode is not a production-release blocker for the current pipeline.

Full byte-resumable transfer jobs are not implemented. | Keep restart recovery terminal and retry-based.
